Priority Recommendations

  1. Add post-quantum crypto to nextgcore-crypt (ML-KEM, ML-DSA)
  2. Add OpenTelemetry alongside Prometheus in nextgcore-metrics
  3. Migrate to full async I/O in nextgcore-core networking modules
  4. Add dynamic config reload to nextgcore-app
  5. Add 6G types to nextgcore-proto (ISAC, NTN, AI/ML, semantic comm)
  6. Add ZUC-256 to nextgcore-crypt for 256-bit security

Contributing to NextGCore

NextGCore is a research-faithful 5G Core and 4G EPC implementation in Rust: a Rel-15 baseline with selected Rel-16/17/18 features (RACS, MBS, NSACF, LMF positioning, RedCap, XR QoS, SNPN, MINT, UAV). 6G/Rel-20 items (ISAC, FL, PQC, semantic, agent-native) are non-normative prototypes — no frozen Rel-20 3GPP specs exist.

Prerequisites

ToolVersionPurpose
Rust1.95.0, pinned by src/rust-toolchain.toml (rustup installs it)Build toolchain
C/C++ compiler, cmakeany recentaws-lc-sys, the C crypto library under rustls
libsctp-devanyonly for --features kernel-sctp (amfd, mmed)
Docker + Compose v2Engine ≥ 24, Compose ≥ 2.20E2E testing
 
# Ubuntu/Debian
sudo apt-get install -y build-essential cmake pkg-config
sudo apt-get install -y libsctp-dev        # kernel-SCTP builds only

# macOS (Homebrew)
brew install cmake

The Docker builder image (docker/rust/Dockerfile.builder) installs a superset (protobuf-compiler, clang, libssl-dev, …) because it also builds nextgsim.

The generated codec crates (nextgcore-*-gen) are memory-hungry to compile: the NR RRC codec alone peaks at about 9.4 GB of rustc RSS with debuginfo, so give cargo test --workspace 16 GB or more, or set CARGO_PROFILE_TEST_DEBUG=0 as CI does.

Building

The workspace root is nextgcore/src/ (not nextgcore/):

cd nextgcore/src
cargo build --workspace            # debug
cargo build --workspace --release  # release

To build a single NF binary:

cargo build -p nextgcore-amfd

Testing

cd nextgcore/src
cargo test --workspace                          # all tests
cargo test -p nextgcore-amfd                    # single NF
cargo test --workspace -- --nocapture           # with log output

src/tests (nextgcore-integration-tests) runs flow tests against mock NFs. The end-to-end tests against the real binaries need Docker:

cd nextgcore/docker/rust
./e2e.sh                     # preflight -> build -> registration, PDU session, ping
./e2e.sh --keep && ./ue-flow-check.sh all   # paging, idle uplink, de/re-registration

Multi-UE stress test:

bash test-multi-ue.sh               # 5 UEs (default)
bash test-multi-ue.sh --ue-count 10 # 10 UEs

Code Style

All code must pass cargo fmt and cargo clippy:

cd nextgcore/src
cargo fmt --all
cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets
cargo check --workspace --all-features --all-targets

CI fails on clippy errors; warnings are reported but do not fail the job. Do not add new warnings to the code you touch.

Pre-commit checklist:

  • cargo fmt --all -- --check — no formatting changes
  • cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets — no errors, no new warnings
  • cargo check --workspace --all-features --all-targets — every feature compiles
  • cargo test --workspace — all tests pass
  • cd fuzz && cargo clippy --locked --bins -- -D warnings — if you touched a decoder the fuzz targets call
  • cargo audit — no unpatched advisories

Tests that touch process-global state (an NF context, an environment variable) must take the lock declared beside that global, so the default parallel cargo test cannot race them.

Security Auditing

cargo install cargo-audit
cd nextgcore/src
cargo audit

└── docs-book/               # mdBook sources

3GPP Specification References

Each file includes a doc comment referencing the 3GPP TS number and section (e.g. //! TS 23.501 §5.15). When adding new protocol logic, always cite the relevant spec:

  • TS 23.501 — 5G system architecture
  • TS 23.502 — 5G procedures
  • TS 29.xxx — SBI service definitions
  • TS 38.331 — NR RRC
  • TS 38.413 — NGAP; TS 36.413 — S1AP
  • TS 24.501 / TS 24.301 — 5GS / EPS NAS

Generated codecs are rebuilt from the ASN.1 in each nextgcore-*-gen/asn/; the version and source are recorded in that directory’s PROVENANCE.md. Do not hand-edit generated code; change the schema or the compiler (NextgCoreLab/asn1-codegen, pinned by rev in src/Cargo.toml).

Commit Messages

Follow conventional commits:

feat(smf): add MBS multicast session context (TS 23.247)
fix(amf): release SMF session on UE context release
test(upf): add SDF filter 5-tuple matching unit tests
  • Never include Co-Authored-By: Claude or any AI attribution
  • Sign commits: Signed-off-by: Your Name <email>

Pull Request Process

  1. Fork the repository
  2. Create a feature branch from main
  3. Implement your change with tests
  4. Ensure all CI checks pass
  5. Open a PR with a description referencing the 3GPP TS item

NF Implementation Standards

Each NF binary must:

  • Register with NRF on startup (via nextgcore-sbi NF management), and deregister on shutdown
  • Expose /metrics (Prometheus, port 9090)
  • Respond to SIGTERM with graceful shutdown
  • Log at info level by default, configurable via --log-level and RUST_LOG (13 NFs honour RUST_LOG today; #691 tracks the rest)
  • Have ≥ 1 unit test per public function
